HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_081fec8c3aff41df93cd","text":"98:8 supplied the decisive later activation: `جَزَاؤُهُمْ` (`ج ز ي` B001) made the intentionality of fixed `ع م ل` B001 causally legible as input to a matching outcome. The gardens’ continuing provision and the mirrored approval also expanded fixed `خ ي ر` B005 from abstract goodness into beneficence that circulates and returns. No further correction was needed to the paired faith-work mechanism; the later ayah specified its realized form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000876/B001","root_000876/B002","root_001046/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0ec7ba47377e03d42dda","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:7:4\",\"root_001046\",[\"B003\"]],[\"98:7:5\",\"root_000876\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İyi işler, yalnızca bireysel erdemler değil, kanıttan sonra bozulan ortak düzeni onaran ve uzlaştıran üstlenilmiş işlerdir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_146fd63978cd0abee30c","text":"4. **Goodness as beneficence that circulates.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `خَيْر` also activates `خ ي ر` B005, “beneficence and gift.” **Structural cue and role:** 98:5 has just made granting growth-purity (`يُؤْتُوا ٱلزَّكَاة`) a core practice, and 98:8 answers the sound actors with recompense, gardens, flow, and approval. The gift branch assigns `خَيْر` an outwardly productive role rather than a merely comparative one. **Abductive move:** I infer that the givers’ beneficence and the later grant form a reciprocal circulation; the packet gives the sequence, not a quid-pro-quo formula here. **Reading change:** the “best” are characterized by good that moves through them, and their outcome continues that movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000099/B002","root_000100/B002","root_000876/B001","root_000876/B002","root_001046/B001","root_001046/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_18815ec97a6c8fd0700a","text":"2. **Righteous works repair the social rupture.** Evidence: ع م ل B001 intentional doing and B003 assigned charge; ص ل ح B001 soundness against corruption and B002 reconciliation; ب ر ء B002 clearance/separation. Structural cue: 98:4 reports communal rupture; 98:5 gives enacted obligations; 98:7 names work that is ṣāliḥ. Assigned role: work turns faith into repair, and ṣalāḥ removes فساد and نفار. Abductive move: I infer that صالحات are acts whose rightness is visible in repaired relations, not merely private merit. Reading change: the believers are “best of creation” because they enact repair after proof, not because belief remains internal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002","root_001046/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2867b68fed4ce4190cae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:7:3\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"98:7:4\",\"root_001046\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İman, açık kanıtı kabul eden yerleşik güvenin amaçlı davranışta görünür hâle gelmesidir; eylem inancın ayrı bir eki değil dışa vurumudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5cf0e0031cc60f512ca3","text":"98:8’s reward “عند ربهم” made خير البرية less comparative and more relational: their goodness is recognized near the Lord who created and nurtured them. The final “لمن خشي ربه” also surprised the īmān reading by making secure trust coexist with reverent fear.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6bd944244919c3e4dad3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:7:3\",\"root_000054\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İmandaki güven, sonraki Rab korkusuyla çelişmez; bilinçli saygıyı paniğe dönüşmekten alıkoyan bir iç yerleşiklik sağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7b5bd8fb3050063c368e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:7:4\",\"root_001046\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Amaçlı işler, sonraki karşılığın biçiminde dış dünyada karşılığını bulan, kişiye yüklenebilir girdiler hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8e7f66ee14ffbbff6c35","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:7:8\",\"root_000452\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İyilik, zekâtla başlayıp bahçeler ve karşılıkla geri dönen, kişiden çevresine geçen üretken bir yarar niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_908e784bb46f13956e25","text":"5. **Intentional acts as legible inputs to a matching outcome.** **Lexical evidence:** `عَمِلُوا` anchors `ع م ل` B001, intentional action; later `جَزَاؤُهُمْ` activates `ج ز ي` B001, recompense matching an act. **Structural cue and role:** the work clause immediately precedes the reward description, while 98:8 makes the outcome possessive (`جَزَاؤُهُمْ`). Intention makes the acts attributable; recompense makes their consequence proportionate. **Abductive move:** I infer a causal ledger from adjacency and the lexical match branch; the packet does not detail how each act maps to each feature of the outcome. **Reading change:** `عَمِلُوا` is not generic activity but accountable, purposive input whose pattern becomes externally realized.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002","root_001046/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a6cd0a1904d9bdd61550","text":"1. **Faith as settled trust made visible by intentional work.** **Lexical evidence:** `ءَامَنُوا` anchors `ء م ن` B002, “trusting assent and confirmation,” with B001, “settled security and trust”; `عَمِلُوا` anchors `ع م ل` B001, “intentional doing and work.” **Structural cue and role:** the two verbs are coordinated before a single classification, after the packet has presented voiced sheets, command, prayer, and giving. Trust supplies inward settlement; work supplies purposive external realization. **Abductive move:** I infer that the coordination makes action the visible continuation of assent rather than an unrelated second credential. **Reading change:** faith reads as reliance stable enough to organize deliberate conduct, not as bare acknowledgment of the proof.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000099/B001","root_000099/B003","root_000100/B001","root_000100/B003","root_000452/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c145ac497675fda69834","text":"3. **Goodness as chosen/beneficial createdness.** Evidence: خ ي ر B001 desired good/opposite evil, B002 chosen excellence, B005 beneficence; ب ر ء B001 creation and B003 recovery. Structural cue: 98:6’s شر البرية is answered by خير البرية. Assigned role: khayr marks both moral opposite and selected excellence; barīyya keeps the creaturely frame. Abductive move: I infer that creation reaches its best state when faith and action align with benefit and repair. Reading change: “best” is not a static rank but a healed, chosen mode of creaturely life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000054/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e0672ab1b1a5a8e90493","text":"1. **Trust stabilizes the heart after fear and division.** Evidence: ء م ن B001 settled security/trust and B002 confirming truth; ف ر ق in 98:4 B009 heart split by fear; خ ش ي in 98:8 B001 reverent fear. Structural cue: the surah moves from division after proof to faith and then fear of the Lord. Assigned role: īmān settles what tafarruq and khashya could scatter. Abductive move: I infer that true fear becomes trust rather than panic when directed to the Lord. Reading change: “believed” is not just assent; it is the heart’s secure reception of the proof.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000099/B001","root_000100/B001","root_000452/B002","root_000452/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fa31140b456c07c2cfbb","text":"3. **Chosen excellence emerging from choices.** **Lexical evidence:** `خَيْر` anchors `خ ي ر` B002, “virtue and chosen excellence,” and B003, “choosing or seeking the better option”; `ٱلْبَرِيَّة` anchors `ب ر ء` B001, the created field. **Structural cue and role:** 98:6–98:7 hold `ٱلْبَرِيَّة` constant while reversing the appraisal, and the present clause inserts faith and work before `خَيْر`. The choice branch turns excellence into the outcome of response. **Abductive move:** I infer that those appraised as choice become so through their choosing under disclosure; the text states deeds before rank but not this lexical causality. **Reading change:** “best of creation” is dynamic excellence selected through enacted response, not a status detached from action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000876/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fb71f8daf860a8b21df6","text":"2. **Sound acts as repair of post-disclosure fragmentation.** **Lexical evidence:** `ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰت` anchors `ص ل ح` B001, “soundness and rightness against corruption,” and activates B002, “reconciliation and settlement between people.” **Structural cue and role:** 98:4 has made social scattering the central post-proof failure, while 98:5 prescribes exclusive orientation, maintained prayer, and material giving. The reconciliation branch assigns sound acts a social repair role in addition to individual rightness. **Abductive move:** I infer that acts opposite the displayed fragmentation include restoring relation; the plural noun does not enumerate reconciliation. **Reading change:** `ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰت` becomes conduct that repairs a damaged shared order, not only a collection of private merits.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
